# Weak pilot: how hard Grovebound is for a slower player (2026-10-22)

**Short version.** The normal test pilot is never in danger on Waylight runs (lowest HP averages 73% of max and never
goes below 41%; 0 losses in 180 runs). Give the same pilot human-like handicaps and the game starts to bite. With a quarter-second
reaction delay and only every second special press taken, it takes about 2.7x the damage, its lowest HP averages 52%,
43% of runs drop below half health, and 5 of 180 runs are lost. With a half-second delay and only every fourth press
taken, 14 of 180 runs are lost and a fifth of runs drop below 25% HP. Nearly every loss happens in Ember Quarry, about
5:00 to 6:30 into the run (one run dies late in Moonfen). Weapon commands help less than you might expect once the
player is slow: a slow pilot that also taps commands (every second chance) still loses 3 of 180 and takes about 4.8x
the damage of the fast commanding pilot. Warden keeps the most health in every weak arm, even though it takes the most damage; Ranger and Ember fall lowest.

**What this means for the Milestone 3 phone session.** If Daniel plays roughly like the 250 ms arm, expect most Waylight
runs to be won but with real scares (about half the runs below 50% HP), and an occasional death in the Quarry. If the
game feels much easier than that on the phone, the bot's handicaps are harsher than a real thumb; if it feels harder,
the Quarry is the place to look first. This is still a bot: it steers by the same rules as the strong pilot, never
panics, and never misreads the screen, so treat it as a lower bound on difficulty for a careful player, not as a human
measurement.

**What the weak pilot is.** The normal debug pilot still makes every decision. A filter in `src/game/pilot.ts`
(`createPilotSkillFilter`) then (1) delays the joystick direction and every press by the reaction time, measured in
game time, and (2) only acts on every Nth "chance", where a chance is one unbroken stretch in which the pilot wants to
press the special (or a given weapon command). It runs only when `scripts/pilot-matrix.mjs` is given `--skill ms:N`;
the game itself never calls it, and the default pilot is unchanged (54 of 54 default runs re-run identical to
`resonance-split-9seeds.json`).

**Slice.** 30 Waylight seeds (w1 to w75, every valid Waylight seed in that range) x 3 heroes x 2 Quarry routes, Moonfen
route fixed to Moonflame: 180 runs per arm, 900 runs total. Each weak run is paired with the default run on the same
seed, hero, and route. Arms:

- `off`: default pilot, no weapon commands (the matrix's standard arm).
- `off-weakR250N2`: 250 ms reaction, every 2nd chance, no weapon commands.
- `off-weakR500N4`: 500 ms reaction, every 4th chance, no weapon commands.
- `cmd`: default pilot that also taps weapon commands (PR #9 policy).
- `cmd-weakR250N2`: the same, through the 250 ms / every-2nd-chance filter.

Numbers are means ± the half-width of a 95% t interval over runs. Lowest HP is health as a share of max health at
that moment, and a lost run counts as 0%. Clear time is not the point here and is left in the per-run table only.

| Arm | Runs | Wins | Lowest HP % | Damage taken | Runs below 50% HP | Runs below 25% HP |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| off (default) | 180 | 180 | 72.7 ± 1.6 | 110 ± 10 | 10 | 0 |
| off-weakR250N2 | 180 | 175 | 51.7 ± 3.2 | 302 ± 19 | 78 | 22 |
| off-weakR500N4 | 180 | 166 | 46.2 ± 3.5 | 349 ± 21 | 89 | 39 |
| cmd (default + commands) | 180 | 180 | 81.0 ± 1.7 | 53 ± 6 | 2 | 0 |
| cmd-weakR250N2 | 180 | 177 | 57.0 ± 2.9 | 252 ± 17 | 58 | 12 |

Paired differences and per-hero breakdowns are in the "Weak pilot" sections below. One weak run
(`ember/.../coolantRun/moonflame@w55+off-weakR500N4`) was flagged as a softlock (129 s without objective progress)
but still won; no other run stalled. One harness page-load timeout was rerun; it does not affect any recorded row.

Reproduce (from the grovebound root; the script regenerates this file's tables below and drops this lead):

```
node scripts/pilot-matrix.mjs --out reports/pilot-matrix/weak-pilot.json --contracts waylight --moon moonflame \
  --seed-set w1,w3,w5,w7,w9,w11,w13,w15,w17,w19,w21,w23,w33,w35,w37,w39,w41,w43,w45,w47,w49,w51,w53,w55,w65,w67,w69,w71,w73,w75 \
  [--commands on] [--skill 250:2 | --skill 500:4] --concurrency 14
```
